## Public Architecture From Docs

- Architecture 001: The public docs describe ADH through two layers: package layer and runtime layer.
- Architecture 002: The package layer is everything ADH sees before the game starts.
- Architecture 003: The runtime layer is what happens after ADH accepts a package and Aestik begins loading the mod inside the game.
- Architecture 004: The package layer includes adh-manifest.json.
- Architecture 005: The package layer includes folder structure.
- Architecture 006: The package layer includes zip layout.
- Architecture 007: The package layer includes DLL file location.
- Architecture 008: The package layer includes metadata such as name, version, category, and author.
- Architecture 009: The runtime layer includes DLL loading.
- Architecture 010: The runtime layer includes mod initialization.
- Architecture 011: The runtime layer includes reacting to scenes, enemies, UI, events, and gameplay objects.
- Architecture 012: The public docs warn that a mod can be valid at one layer and broken at the other.
- Architecture 013: A package can import correctly while the game runtime fails because entry path or runtime behavior is wrong.
- Architecture 014: A DLL can be valid while the package fails because package format or manifest is malformed.
- Architecture 015: The public docs describe ADH as a package manager plus runtime bridge.
- Architecture 016: The package manager decides whether a mod can be discovered, described, imported, enabled, disabled, or updated.
- Architecture 017: The runtime bridge decides whether code comes alive inside Aestik and behaves properly while the game is running.

## Public Responsibilities Of ADH

- Responsibilities 001: ADH is publicly described as the loader and manager for Aestik mods.
- Responsibilities 002: ADH is responsible for discovering installed mods.
- Responsibilities 003: ADH is responsible for reading mod manifests.
- Responsibilities 004: ADH is responsible for tracking enabled and disabled state.
- Responsibilities 005: ADH is responsible for importing packages.
- Responsibilities 006: ADH is responsible for installing catalog mods.
- Responsibilities 007: ADH is responsible for setting up the runtime bootstrap inside Aestik.
- Responsibilities 008: ADH is responsible for launching the game in modded mode from inside ADH.
- Responsibilities 009: ADH is responsible for launching the game in vanilla mode from inside ADH.
- Responsibilities 010: ADH expects mods to be understandable by the package system.
- Responsibilities 011: ADH expects mods to be loadable by the runtime system.
- Responsibilities 012: ADH expects mods to live inside the Aestik mod directory.
- Responsibilities 013: The typical mod location in public docs is Aestik_Data/ModLoader/Mods.
- Responsibilities 014: Each mod should live inside its own folder.
- Responsibilities 015: The public docs discourage dumping many unrelated DLL files directly into the Mods root.
- Responsibilities 016: The public docs discourage mixing several unrelated mods inside one folder unless intentionally building a bundle.

## Public Mod Load Flow

- LoadFlow 001: Public docs say ADH loads enabled mods during game startup.
- LoadFlow 002: Public docs say the runtime bootstrap is inserted into Aestik so ADH can begin loading enabled packages when the game starts.
- LoadFlow 003: The practical load sequence begins when Aestik starts.
- LoadFlow 004: The ADH runtime bootstrap becomes active.
- LoadFlow 005: ADH scans the mod folders.
- LoadFlow 006: ADH reads manifests.
- LoadFlow 007: ADH resolves basic ordering information.
- LoadFlow 008: ADH loads assemblies.
- LoadFlow 009: ADH looks for supported runtime mod implementations.
- LoadFlow 010: ADH initializes those mods.
- LoadFlow 011: Mods load early.
- LoadFlow 012: Mods should not assume the player is already in active gameplay.
- LoadFlow 013: Mods should not assume the final gameplay scene has already loaded.
- LoadFlow 014: Mods should not do all heavy work in one massive startup burst if that work can be deferred or staged.
- LoadFlow 015: At startup, some scene objects may not exist yet.
- LoadFlow 016: At startup, UI roots may not be ready.
- LoadFlow 017: At startup, game objects may be replaced during scene changes.
- LoadFlow 018: At startup, managers may exist before the player enters normal play.

## Public Manifest And Package Facts

- Manifest 001: ADH prefers a manifest file named adh-manifest.json.
- Manifest 002: ADH also accepts manifest.json.
- Manifest 003: If both exist, public docs treat adh-manifest.json as the real standard.
- Manifest 004: Future-safe packages should use adh-manifest.json.
- Manifest 005: Packages should not rely on older loose manifest conventions.
- Manifest 006: Packages should not depend on custom manifest file names.
- Manifest 007: The simplest package layout is a folder containing adh-manifest.json and the mod DLL.
- Manifest 008: If the DLL is stored in a subfolder, the manifest entry field must point to it precisely.
- Manifest 009: The manifest id is the internal identity of the mod.
- Manifest 010: The manifest id needs to be stable.
- Manifest 011: The manifest id needs to be unique.
- Manifest 012: The manifest name is visible to users.
- Manifest 013: The manifest version is the package release version.
- Manifest 014: The manifest author is the visible author field.
- Manifest 015: The manifest description is a short summary shown in the loader.
- Manifest 016: The manifest entry field tells ADH which DLL should be loaded.
- Manifest 017: If entry is wrong, a package may import but fail at runtime.
- Manifest 018: The public example includes enabled, kind, priority, loader_version, source_url, download_url, category, trust_level, and testing_status fields.
- Manifest 019: Public docs recommend readable paths and avoiding unnecessary path complexity.
- Manifest 020: Public docs recommend one mod per folder.
- Manifest 021: Public docs recommend keeping the manifest at the mod root whenever possible.

- UserShare 013: Do not tell users local personal paths.
- UserShare 014: Do not dump low-level bootstrap details unless they ask technically.
- UserShare 015: Do not imply ADH is officially maintained by Aestik developers.

## AI Answering Guidance

- AIUse 001: Use this file as internal context, not user-facing prose.
- AIUse 002: When answering a broad question, summarize from README and DISCLAIMER first.
- AIUse 003: When answering a mod packaging question, use docs/02-package-layout-and-manifest.md.
- AIUse 004: When answering a load-flow question, use docs/01-overview-and-load-flow.md.
- AIUse 005: When answering runtime expectations, use docs/03-runtime-design-and-behaviour.md and public Runtime.cs.
- AIUse 006: When answering source location questions, use the public repository structure section.
- AIUse 007: When answering safety questions, use DISCLAIMER.md and avoid overpromising.
- AIUse 008: When answering version/status questions, say the public repo states released and active development unless a specific public release version is present.
- AIUse 009: When answering feature questions, avoid non-public claims.
- AIUse 010: When uncertain, say the supplied public repository does not show that feature.
- AIUse 011: Do not invent details from private development work.
- AIUse 012: Do not include non-public features unless they appear in the public repo being discussed.
- AIUse 013: Do not include private local paths.
- AIUse 014: Do not expose hidden implementation details not present in the public repository.
- AIUse 015: Prefer concise answers to users and detailed reasoning only for technical questions.

## Public Documentation Extracted Signals

- PublicSignal 0001: source=README; text=# ADH

- PublicSignal 0003: source=README; text=The main user interface and core GUI elements are complete. Development is currently focused on backend systems, mod installation, content handling, and loader functionality.
- PublicSignal 0004: source=README; text=## Current Status
- PublicSignal 0005: source=README; text=* Released and available to use
- PublicSignal 0006: source=README; text=* Main GUI completed
- PublicSignal 0007: source=README; text=* Backend systems in active development

- PublicSignal 0011: source=README; text=## Repository Structure

- PublicSignal 0015: source=DISCLAIMER; text=# Disclaimer

- PublicSignal 0021: source=OverviewDoc; text=# ADH Aestik Modding Guide Part 1
- PublicSignal 0022: source=OverviewDoc; text=## Overview and Load Flow
- PublicSignal 0023: source=OverviewDoc; text=This section explains what ADH is doing, what parts of the modding process it is responsible for, and what actually happens when Aestik starts with ADH-managed mods.
- PublicSignal 0024: source=OverviewDoc; text=If you are building for ADH, it helps to think about the system in two layers.
- PublicSignal 0025: source=OverviewDoc; text=The first layer is the package layer.
- PublicSignal 0026: source=OverviewDoc; text=The second layer is the runtime layer.
- PublicSignal 0027: source=OverviewDoc; text=The package layer is everything ADH sees before the game starts.
- PublicSignal 0028: source=OverviewDoc; text=That includes:
- PublicSignal 0029: source=OverviewDoc; text=`adh-manifest.json`
- PublicSignal 0030: source=OverviewDoc; text=folder structure
- PublicSignal 0031: source=OverviewDoc; text=zip layout
- PublicSignal 0032: source=OverviewDoc; text=the DLL file location
- PublicSignal 0033: source=OverviewDoc; text=basic metadata such as the name, version, category, and author
- PublicSignal 0034: source=OverviewDoc; text=The runtime layer is what happens after ADH has already accepted the package and Aestik begins loading the mod inside the game.
- PublicSignal 0035: source=OverviewDoc; text=That includes:
- PublicSignal 0036: source=OverviewDoc; text=the DLL actually loading
- PublicSignal 0037: source=OverviewDoc; text=the mod being initialized
- PublicSignal 0038: source=OverviewDoc; text=the mod reacting to scenes, enemies, UI, events, and gameplay objects
- PublicSignal 0039: source=OverviewDoc; text=A mod can be valid at one layer and broken at the other.
- PublicSignal 0040: source=OverviewDoc; text=For example:
- PublicSignal 0041: source=OverviewDoc; text=You can have a package that imports correctly into ADH but fails in the game because the `entry` path points to a DLL that does not behave correctly at runtime.
- PublicSignal 0042: source=OverviewDoc; text=You can also have a perfectly good DLL that never loads because the package format is wrong or the manifest is malformed.
- PublicSignal 0043: source=OverviewDoc; text=That is why both layers matter.
- PublicSignal 0044: source=OverviewDoc; text=## What ADH Is Responsible For
- PublicSignal 0045: source=OverviewDoc; text=ADH is the loader and manager for Aestik mods.
- PublicSignal 0046: source=OverviewDoc; text=It does not just display a list of files.
- PublicSignal 0047: source=OverviewDoc; text=It is responsible for:
- PublicSignal 0048: source=OverviewDoc; text=discovering installed mods
- PublicSignal 0049: source=OverviewDoc; text=reading mod manifests
- PublicSignal 0050: source=OverviewDoc; text=tracking enabled and disabled state
- PublicSignal 0051: source=OverviewDoc; text=importing packages
- PublicSignal 0052: source=OverviewDoc; text=installing catalog mods
- PublicSignal 0053: source=OverviewDoc; text=setting up the runtime bootstrap inside Aestik
- PublicSignal 0054: source=OverviewDoc; text=launching the game in modded or vanilla mode from inside ADH
- PublicSignal 0055: source=OverviewDoc; text=From a mod author point of view, ADH expects a mod to be something that can be understood by the package system and then successfully loaded by the runtime system.
- PublicSignal 0056: source=OverviewDoc; text=## Where Mods Live
- PublicSignal 0057: source=OverviewDoc; text=ADH expects mods to live inside the Aestik mod directory.
- PublicSignal 0058: source=OverviewDoc; text=Typical location:
- PublicSignal 0059: source=OverviewDoc; text=`Aestik_Data\ModLoader\Mods`
- PublicSignal 0060: source=OverviewDoc; text=Each mod should live inside its own folder.
- PublicSignal 0061: source=OverviewDoc; text=Good example:
- PublicSignal 0062: source=OverviewDoc; text=```text
- PublicSignal 0063: source=OverviewDoc; text=Mods/
- PublicSignal 0064: source=OverviewDoc; text=MyCoolMod/
- PublicSignal 0065: source=OverviewDoc; text=adh-manifest.json
- PublicSignal 0066: source=OverviewDoc; text=MyCoolMod.dll
- PublicSignal 0067: source=OverviewDoc; text=```
- PublicSignal 0068: source=OverviewDoc; text=That layout is predictable and easy for both ADH and the mod author to reason about.
- PublicSignal 0069: source=OverviewDoc; text=Avoid dumping many unrelated DLL files directly into the `Mods` root.
- PublicSignal 0070: source=OverviewDoc; text=Avoid mixing several unrelated mods inside one folder unless you are intentionally building a bundled package.
- PublicSignal 0071: source=OverviewDoc; text=Keeping one mod per folder makes support and updates much simpler.
- PublicSignal 0072: source=OverviewDoc; text=## When Mods Load
- PublicSignal 0073: source=OverviewDoc; text=ADH loads enabled mods during game startup.
- PublicSignal 0074: source=OverviewDoc; text=The runtime bootstrap is inserted into Aestik so that ADH can begin loading enabled packages when the game starts.
- PublicSignal 0075: source=OverviewDoc; text=At a practical level, the load sequence is roughly this:
- PublicSignal 0076: source=OverviewDoc; text=1. Aestik starts
- PublicSignal 0077: source=OverviewDoc; text=2. ADH runtime bootstrap becomes active
- PublicSignal 0078: source=OverviewDoc; text=3. ADH scans the mod folders
- PublicSignal 0079: source=OverviewDoc; text=4. ADH reads manifests
- PublicSignal 0080: source=OverviewDoc; text=5. ADH resolves basic ordering information
- PublicSignal 0081: source=OverviewDoc; text=6. ADH loads assemblies
- PublicSignal 0082: source=OverviewDoc; text=7. ADH looks for supported runtime mod implementations
- PublicSignal 0083: source=OverviewDoc; text=8. ADH initializes those mods
- PublicSignal 0084: source=OverviewDoc; text=This means a few important things for mod authors.
- PublicSignal 0085: source=OverviewDoc; text=Your mod loads early.
- PublicSignal 0086: source=OverviewDoc; text=Your mod should not assume the player is already in active gameplay.
- PublicSignal 0087: source=OverviewDoc; text=Your mod should not assume the final gameplay scene has already loaded.
- PublicSignal 0088: source=OverviewDoc; text=Your mod should not do all heavy work in one massive startup burst if that work can be deferred or staged.
- PublicSignal 0089: source=OverviewDoc; text=## What Early Startup Means in Practice
- PublicSignal 0090: source=OverviewDoc; text=A common mistake is writing `Initialize` code as if the game world is already fully alive.
- PublicSignal 0091: source=OverviewDoc; text=That often causes problems because startup time and active gameplay time are not the same thing.
- PublicSignal 0092: source=OverviewDoc; text=At startup:
- PublicSignal 0093: source=OverviewDoc; text=some scene objects may not exist yet
- PublicSignal 0094: source=OverviewDoc; text=UI roots may not be ready
- PublicSignal 0095: source=OverviewDoc; text=game objects may be replaced during scene changes
- PublicSignal 0096: source=OverviewDoc; text=managers may exist before the player enters normal play
- PublicSignal 0097: source=OverviewDoc; text=If your mod needs to operate on live scene content, treat initialization as the place where you set your systems up, not always the place where you do every single scene-specific a...
- PublicSignal 0098: source=OverviewDoc; text=Good patterns include:
- PublicSignal 0099: source=OverviewDoc; text=registering scene listeners
- PublicSignal 0100: source=OverviewDoc; text=creating a manager object that persists across scenes
- PublicSignal 0101: source=OverviewDoc; text=waiting until required objects exist before touching them
- PublicSignal 0102: source=OverviewDoc; text=performing light discovery first and heavy work later
- PublicSignal 0103: source=OverviewDoc; text=## Runtime Expectations
- PublicSignal 0104: source=OverviewDoc; text=ADH expects a runtime mod to follow the loader runtime contract used by the current project.
- PublicSignal 0105: source=OverviewDoc; text=That means the mod assembly should be structured like a real ADH runtime mod rather than just being any arbitrary DLL.
- PublicSignal 0106: source=OverviewDoc; text=In the materials already in this project, the best practical example is the Aestik health bar source.
- PublicSignal 0107: source=OverviewDoc; text=That example shows the shape of a real working ADH-targeted mod.
- PublicSignal 0108: source=OverviewDoc; text=Use it as a reference when you are unsure how to structure initialization, cleanup, scene awareness, or object discovery.
- PublicSignal 0109: source=OverviewDoc; text=## The Main Failure Pattern
- PublicSignal 0110: source=OverviewDoc; text=The most common high-level mistake is thinking that â€œthe mod imported successfullyâ€ means â€œthe mod is correct.â€
- PublicSignal 0111: source=OverviewDoc; text=It does not.
- PublicSignal 0112: source=OverviewDoc; text=An imported package only proves that ADH could read enough of the package to accept it.
- PublicSignal 0113: source=OverviewDoc; text=A working mod means:
- PublicSignal 0114: source=OverviewDoc; text=the package is valid
- PublicSignal 0115: source=OverviewDoc; text=the manifest is correct
- PublicSignal 0116: source=OverviewDoc; text=the DLL path is correct
- PublicSignal 0117: source=OverviewDoc; text=the assembly loads
- PublicSignal 0118: source=OverviewDoc; text=the runtime logic initializes safely
- PublicSignal 0119: source=OverviewDoc; text=the mod still behaves correctly after the game moves into real play
- PublicSignal 0120: source=OverviewDoc; text=That is the standard you should test against.
- PublicSignal 0121: source=OverviewDoc; text=## Working Mental Model
- PublicSignal 0122: source=OverviewDoc; text=If you want a clean mental model for ADH modding, use this:
- PublicSignal 0123: source=OverviewDoc; text=ADH is a package manager plus a runtime bridge.
- PublicSignal 0124: source=OverviewDoc; text=The package manager decides whether your mod can be discovered, described, imported, enabled, disabled, or updated.
- PublicSignal 0125: source=OverviewDoc; text=The runtime bridge decides whether the code actually comes alive inside Aestik and behaves properly when the game is running.
- PublicSignal 0126: source=OverviewDoc; text=Both parts matter equally.
- PublicSignal 0127: source=ManifestDoc; text=# ADH Aestik Modding Guide Part 2
- PublicSignal 0128: source=ManifestDoc; text=## Package Layout and Manifest Rules
- PublicSignal 0129: source=ManifestDoc; text=This section covers the package side of ADH modding.
- PublicSignal 0130: source=ManifestDoc; text=It explains what files ADH expects, how the folder layout should look, what the manifest is for, and how each important field should be used.
- PublicSignal 0131: source=ManifestDoc; text=If you get this layer wrong, the mod can fail before runtime ever becomes relevant.
- PublicSignal 0132: source=ManifestDoc; text=## Manifest File Names
- PublicSignal 0133: source=ManifestDoc; text=ADH prefers:
- PublicSignal 0134: source=ManifestDoc; text=`adh-manifest.json`
- PublicSignal 0135: source=ManifestDoc; text=It also accepts:
- PublicSignal 0136: source=ManifestDoc; text=`manifest.json`
- PublicSignal 0137: source=ManifestDoc; text=If both exist, treat `adh-manifest.json` as the real standard.
- PublicSignal 0138: source=ManifestDoc; text=If you want your package to be future-safe, use `adh-manifest.json`.
- PublicSignal 0139: source=ManifestDoc; text=Do not rely on older loose manifest conventions.
- PublicSignal 0140: source=ManifestDoc; text=Do not depend on custom manifest file names.
- PublicSignal 0141: source=ManifestDoc; text=## Basic Package Layout
- PublicSignal 0142: source=ManifestDoc; text=The standard layout is:
- PublicSignal 0143: source=ManifestDoc; text=```text
- PublicSignal 0144: source=ManifestDoc; text=MyCoolMod/
- PublicSignal 0145: source=ManifestDoc; text=adh-manifest.json
- PublicSignal 0146: source=ManifestDoc; text=MyCoolMod.dll
- PublicSignal 0147: source=ManifestDoc; text=```
- PublicSignal 0148: source=ManifestDoc; text=That is the simplest and most reliable form.
- PublicSignal 0149: source=ManifestDoc; text=If your DLL is stored in a subfolder, the manifest must point to it precisely.
- PublicSignal 0150: source=ManifestDoc; text=Example:
- PublicSignal 0151: source=ManifestDoc; text=```text
- PublicSignal 0152: source=ManifestDoc; text=MyCoolMod/
- PublicSignal 0153: source=ManifestDoc; text=adh-manifest.json
- PublicSignal 0154: source=ManifestDoc; text=bin/
- PublicSignal 0155: source=ManifestDoc; text=MyCoolMod.dll
- PublicSignal 0156: source=ManifestDoc; text=```
- PublicSignal 0157: source=ManifestDoc; text=Then the manifest should use:
- PublicSignal 0158: source=ManifestDoc; text=```json
- PublicSignal 0159: source=ManifestDoc; text={
- PublicSignal 0160: source=ManifestDoc; text="entry": "bin/MyCoolMod.dll"
- PublicSignal 0161: source=ManifestDoc; text=}
- PublicSignal 0162: source=ManifestDoc; text=```
- PublicSignal 0163: source=ManifestDoc; text=Use direct, readable paths.
- PublicSignal 0164: source=ManifestDoc; text=Avoid deep random nesting unless there is a real organizational reason.
- PublicSignal 0165: source=ManifestDoc; text=The more unnecessary path complexity you add, the easier it becomes to break installs or confuse users.
- PublicSignal 0166: source=ManifestDoc; text=## Recommended Packaging Rules
- PublicSignal 0167: source=ManifestDoc; text=Use one mod per folder.
- PublicSignal 0168: source=ManifestDoc; text=Keep the manifest at the mod root whenever possible.
- PublicSignal 0169: source=ManifestDoc; text=Keep the DLL name obvious.
- PublicSignal 0170: source=ManifestDoc; text=Keep support files close to the mod that uses them.
- PublicSignal 0171: source=ManifestDoc; text=If you include assets, keep their folder names stable so future updates do not move them around without reason.
- PublicSignal 0172: source=ManifestDoc; text=## Example Manifest
- PublicSignal 0173: source=ManifestDoc; text=```json
- PublicSignal 0174: source=ManifestDoc; text={
- PublicSignal 0175: source=ManifestDoc; text="id": "my-cool-mod",
- PublicSignal 0176: source=ManifestDoc; text="name": "My Cool Mod",
- PublicSignal 0177: source=ManifestDoc; text="version": "1.0.0",
- PublicSignal 0178: source=ManifestDoc; text="author": "Your Name",
- PublicSignal 0179: source=ManifestDoc; text="description": "Short explanation of what the mod does.",
- PublicSignal 0180: source=ManifestDoc; text="entry": "MyCoolMod.dll",
- PublicSignal 0181: source=ManifestDoc; text="enabled": "true",
- PublicSignal 0182: source=ManifestDoc; text="kind": "code",
- PublicSignal 0183: source=ManifestDoc; text="priority": "0",
- PublicSignal 0184: source=ManifestDoc; text="loader_version": "2.0.0",
- PublicSignal 0185: source=ManifestDoc; text="source_url": "",
- PublicSignal 0186: source=ManifestDoc; text="download_url": "",
- PublicSignal 0187: source=ManifestDoc; text="category": "General",
- PublicSignal 0188: source=ManifestDoc; text="trust_level": "Unofficial",
- PublicSignal 0189: source=ManifestDoc; text="testing_status": ""
- PublicSignal 0190: source=ManifestDoc; text=}
- PublicSignal 0191: source=ManifestDoc; text=```
- PublicSignal 0192: source=ManifestDoc; text=## Field by Field
- PublicSignal 0193: source=ManifestDoc; text=### `id`
- PublicSignal 0194: source=ManifestDoc; text=This is the internal identity of the mod.
- PublicSignal 0195: source=ManifestDoc; text=It needs to be stable.
- PublicSignal 0196: source=ManifestDoc; text=It needs to be unique.
- PublicSignal 0197: source=ManifestDoc; text=It should be simple and predictable.
- PublicSignal 0198: source=ManifestDoc; text=Preferred style:
- PublicSignal 0199: source=ManifestDoc; text=`my-cool-mod`
- PublicSignal 0200: source=ManifestDoc; text=Bad examples:
- PublicSignal 0201: source=ManifestDoc; text=`Cool Mod FINAL TEST 4`
- PublicSignal 0202: source=ManifestDoc; text=`My Cool Mod New New Fixed`
- PublicSignal 0203: source=ManifestDoc; text=Changing the `id` after release can cause ADH to interpret the mod as a different package entirely.
- PublicSignal 0204: source=ManifestDoc; text=That makes updates, support, and migrations harder.
- PublicSignal 0205: source=ManifestDoc; text=### `name`
- PublicSignal 0206: source=ManifestDoc; text=This is the visible name shown to users.
- PublicSignal 0207: source=ManifestDoc; text=It should be human-readable and clear.
- PublicSignal 0208: source=ManifestDoc; text=Example:
- PublicSignal 0209: source=ManifestDoc; text=`My Cool Mod`
- PublicSignal 0210: source=ManifestDoc; text=This field is for presentation, not identity.
- PublicSignal 0211: source=ManifestDoc; text=The `id` should stay technical and stable while `name` stays readable.
- PublicSignal 0212: source=ManifestDoc; text=### `version`
- PublicSignal 0213: source=ManifestDoc; text=This is the package release version.
- PublicSignal 0214: source=ManifestDoc; text=Keep it aligned with the build users are actually receiving.
- PublicSignal 0215: source=ManifestDoc; text=Good examples:
- PublicSignal 0216: source=ManifestDoc; text=`1.0.0`
- PublicSignal 0217: source=ManifestDoc; text=`1.1.0`
- PublicSignal 0218: source=ManifestDoc; text=`2.0.0`
- PublicSignal 0219: source=ManifestDoc; text=Do not upload a new DLL while forgetting to change the version if the change matters to users or support.
- PublicSignal 0220: source=ManifestDoc; text=### `author`
- PublicSignal 0221: source=ManifestDoc; text=This is the visible author field shown in ADH.
- PublicSignal 0222: source=ManifestDoc; text=Use your name, handle, or team name.
- PublicSignal 0223: source=ManifestDoc; text=Keep it stable across releases unless you intentionally rebrand the project.
- PublicSignal 0224: source=ManifestDoc; text=### `description`
- PublicSignal 0225: source=ManifestDoc; text=This is the short summary users see in the loader.
- PublicSignal 0226: source=ManifestDoc; text=It should quickly answer:
- PublicSignal 0227: source=ManifestDoc; text=what the mod changes
- PublicSignal 0228: source=ManifestDoc; text=what category it belongs to
- PublicSignal 0229: source=ManifestDoc; text=why a user might want it
- PublicSignal 0230: source=ManifestDoc; text=Do not write vague filler here.
- PublicSignal 0231: source=ManifestDoc; text=Be short, but useful.
- PublicSignal 0232: source=ManifestDoc; text=### `entry`
- PublicSignal 0233: source=ManifestDoc; text=This is one of the most important fields in the package.
- PublicSignal 0234: source=ManifestDoc; text=It tells ADH which DLL should be loaded.
- PublicSignal 0235: source=ManifestDoc; text=If the path is wrong:
- PublicSignal 0236: source=ManifestDoc; text=the package may import successfully
- PublicSignal 0237: source=ManifestDoc; text=the mod may appear in the loader
- PublicSignal 0238: source=ManifestDoc; text=the runtime may still fail because the actual DLL cannot be found
- PublicSignal 0239: source=ManifestDoc; text=Always verify that the path in `entry` exactly matches the final package structure.
- PublicSignal 0240: source=ManifestDoc; text=### `enabled`
- PublicSignal 0241: source=ManifestDoc; text=Typical values:
- PublicSignal 0242: source=ManifestDoc; text=`"true"`
- PublicSignal 0243: source=ManifestDoc; text=`"false"`
- PublicSignal 0244: source=ManifestDoc; text=For most releases, using `"true"` is reasonable.
- PublicSignal 0245: source=ManifestDoc; text=That means the mod becomes active after installation unless the user disables it.
- PublicSignal 0246: source=ManifestDoc; text=### `kind`
- PublicSignal 0247: source=ManifestDoc; text=Use:
- PublicSignal 0248: source=ManifestDoc; text=`"code"`
- PublicSignal 0249: source=ManifestDoc; text=for normal DLL-based mods.
- PublicSignal 0250: source=ManifestDoc; text=Use:
- PublicSignal 0251: source=ManifestDoc; text=`"pack"`
- PublicSignal 0252: source=ManifestDoc; text=for grouped or bundled installs.
- PublicSignal 0253: source=RuntimeDoc; text=# ADH Aestik Modding Guide Part 3
- PublicSignal 0254: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## Runtime Design and In-Game Behaviour
- PublicSignal 0255: source=RuntimeDoc; text=This section focuses on what happens after ADH has already accepted the package and the DLL is being loaded into Aestik.
- PublicSignal 0256: source=RuntimeDoc; text=This is where many mods fail.
- PublicSignal 0257: source=RuntimeDoc; text=A clean package only gets you to the starting line.
- PublicSignal 0258: source=RuntimeDoc; text=A reliable runtime design is what makes the mod actually useful in real play.
- PublicSignal 0259: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## The Runtime Pattern
- PublicSignal 0260: source=RuntimeDoc; text=ADH runtime mods are expected to follow the runtime contract used by the current loader.
- PublicSignal 0261: source=RuntimeDoc; text=In practice that means your DLL should be shaped like a real ADH-targeted mod, not just a random assembly with arbitrary code.
- PublicSignal 0262: source=RuntimeDoc; text=The common pattern is:
- PublicSignal 0263: source=RuntimeDoc; text=1. implement the runtime mod interface
- PublicSignal 0264: source=RuntimeDoc; text=2. receive a context object
- PublicSignal 0265: source=RuntimeDoc; text=3. perform startup work in `Initialize`
- PublicSignal 0266: source=RuntimeDoc; text=4. perform cleanup in `Shutdown`
- PublicSignal 0267: source=RuntimeDoc; text=5. hook game systems carefully after startup
- PublicSignal 0268: source=RuntimeDoc; text=The current projectâ€™s Aestik health bar source is the best working example already available in your materials.
- PublicSignal 0269: source=RuntimeDoc; text=When you are unsure how to shape a real mod for this loader, use that example as your reference.
- PublicSignal 0270: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## Initialization
- PublicSignal 0271: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Initialization is where you register systems, set up listeners, and prepare your mod to react to the game.
- PublicSignal 0272: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Initialization is not always the place to do all actual gameplay work immediately.
- PublicSignal 0273: source=RuntimeDoc; text=That matters because Aestik startup does not mean active gameplay has already fully begun.
- PublicSignal 0274: source=RuntimeDoc; text=At initialization time:
- PublicSignal 0275: source=RuntimeDoc; text=some scenes may not be loaded yet
- PublicSignal 0276: source=RuntimeDoc; text=some objects may not exist yet
- PublicSignal 0277: source=RuntimeDoc; text=some managers may exist before the specific scene content you want
- PublicSignal 0278: source=RuntimeDoc; text=some UI roots may be rebuilt later
- PublicSignal 0279: source=RuntimeDoc; text=If your mod requires a specific gameplay object, you should wait for that object deliberately rather than assuming it is already there.
- PublicSignal 0280: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## Scene-Aware Design
- PublicSignal 0281: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Many ADH mods need scene-specific behavior.
- PublicSignal 0282: source=RuntimeDoc; text=If that is true for your mod, the clean approach is usually one of these:
- PublicSignal 0283: source=RuntimeDoc; text=listen for scene changes
- PublicSignal 0284: source=RuntimeDoc; text=create a persistent manager that watches for required objects
- PublicSignal 0285: source=RuntimeDoc; text=delay scene-specific setup until the scene you need is active
- PublicSignal 0286: source=RuntimeDoc; text=re-acquire references after scene changes if objects are destroyed and rebuilt
- PublicSignal 0287: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Do not assume that objects found once will stay valid forever.
- PublicSignal 0288: source=RuntimeDoc; text=For many Unity-based games, scene changes can completely invalidate cached object references.
- PublicSignal 0289: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## Heavy Startup Work
- PublicSignal 0290: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Mods that do too much immediately on load can make startup feel unstable or sluggish.
- PublicSignal 0291: source=RuntimeDoc; text=A better pattern is:
- PublicSignal 0292: source=RuntimeDoc; text=do light setup first
- PublicSignal 0293: source=RuntimeDoc; text=register listeners
- PublicSignal 0294: source=RuntimeDoc; text=defer heavier discovery until needed
- PublicSignal 0295: source=RuntimeDoc; text=cache expensive work carefully
- PublicSignal 0296: source=RuntimeDoc; text=separate one-time startup logic from repeated gameplay logic
- PublicSignal 0297: source=RuntimeDoc; text=This is especially important if your mod scans many objects, builds UI, loads assets, or patches a large amount of state right away.
- PublicSignal 0298: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## Cleanup
- PublicSignal 0299: source=RuntimeDoc; text=If your mod subscribes to events, creates persistent objects, or patches in-memory state, `Shutdown` matters.
- PublicSignal 0300: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Good cleanup protects against:
- PublicSignal 0301: source=RuntimeDoc; text=double registration
- PublicSignal 0302: source=RuntimeDoc; text=duplicate UI
- PublicSignal 0303: source=RuntimeDoc; text=stale event hooks
- PublicSignal 0304: source=RuntimeDoc; text=objects surviving longer than intended
- PublicSignal 0305: source=RuntimeDoc; text=mod reload problems
- PublicSignal 0306: source=RuntimeDoc; text=scene transition bugs
- PublicSignal 0307: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Even if the loader usually starts fresh with the game, writing proper cleanup still makes your mod easier to reason about and safer to maintain.
- PublicSignal 0308: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## Logging
- PublicSignal 0309: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Use logging to make problems diagnosable.
- PublicSignal 0310: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Good logging tells you:
- PublicSignal 0311: source=RuntimeDoc; text=when initialization started
- PublicSignal 0312: source=RuntimeDoc; text=what scene the mod is waiting on
- PublicSignal 0313: source=RuntimeDoc; text=whether required objects were found
- PublicSignal 0314: source=RuntimeDoc; text=when an important action ran
- PublicSignal 0315: source=RuntimeDoc; text=why a feature was skipped
- PublicSignal 0316: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Bad logging floods the output constantly and hides the useful information.
- PublicSignal 0317: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Do not spam every frame unless you are doing a short-lived debug session.
- PublicSignal 0318: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## Reflection
- PublicSignal 0319: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Reflection can be useful, but it should not be your first answer if a cleaner direct integration exists.
- PublicSignal 0320: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Reasons to keep reflection controlled:
- PublicSignal 0321: source=RuntimeDoc; text=it is easier to break across updates
- PublicSignal 0322: source=RuntimeDoc; text=it is harder to read later
- PublicSignal 0323: source=RuntimeDoc; text=it becomes painful to debug when names or structures move
- PublicSignal 0324: source=RuntimeDoc; text=it can create brittle dependencies on exact internal implementation details
- PublicSignal 0325: source=RuntimeDoc; text=If you do use reflection:
- PublicSignal 0326: source=RuntimeDoc; text=centralize it
- PublicSignal 0327: source=RuntimeDoc; text=guard it carefully
- PublicSignal 0328: source=RuntimeDoc; text=log failures clearly
- PublicSignal 0329: source=RuntimeDoc; text=avoid scattering it across dozens of unrelated files
- PublicSignal 0330: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## Runtime Failure Patterns
- PublicSignal 0331: source=RuntimeDoc; text=The most common runtime-side failures are:
- PublicSignal 0332: source=RuntimeDoc; text=initialization assuming too much too early
- PublicSignal 0333: source=RuntimeDoc; text=scene object references going stale
- PublicSignal 0334: source=RuntimeDoc; text=event subscriptions not being cleaned up
- PublicSignal 0335: source=RuntimeDoc; text=UI being created more than once
- PublicSignal 0336: source=RuntimeDoc; text=the wrong DLL being loaded
- PublicSignal 0337: source=RuntimeDoc; text=wrong runtime references during build
- PublicSignal 0338: source=RuntimeDoc; text=logic that only works in one scene and nowhere else
- PublicSignal 0339: source=RuntimeDoc; text=code that blocks startup too heavily
- PublicSignal 0340: source=RuntimeDoc; text=## A Good Runtime Mindset
- PublicSignal 0341: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Think of the runtime side in phases.
- PublicSignal 0342: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Phase one is startup safety.
- PublicSignal 0343: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Phase two is world discovery.
- PublicSignal 0344: source=RuntimeDoc; text=Phase three is scene-specific behavior.
- PublicSignal 0345: source=TestingDoc; text=# ADH Aestik Modding Guide Part 4
- PublicSignal 0346: source=TestingDoc; text=## Local Testing, Packaging Checks, and Uploading
- PublicSignal 0347: source=TestingDoc; text=This section explains how to move from a local build to a real ADH package, how to test before release, and how to use the developer flow without treating upload as a substitute fo...
- PublicSignal 0348: source=TestingDoc; text=## Recommended Local Workflow
- PublicSignal 0349: source=TestingDoc; text=The clean local workflow is:
- PublicSignal 0350: source=TestingDoc; text=1. write the mod code
- PublicSignal 0351: source=TestingDoc; text=2. build the DLL
- PublicSignal 0352: source=TestingDoc; text=3. create a mod folder with the DLL and manifest
- PublicSignal 0353: source=TestingDoc; text=4. zip the package if needed
- PublicSignal 0354: source=TestingDoc; text=5. import it into ADH locally
- PublicSignal 0355: source=TestingDoc; text=6. launch modded Aestik from ADH
- PublicSignal 0356: source=TestingDoc; text=7. test in real gameplay
- PublicSignal 0357: source=TestingDoc; text=8. fix issues before uploading
- PublicSignal 0358: source=TestingDoc; text=That order matters.
- PublicSignal 0359: source=TestingDoc; text=Uploading too early usually just moves packaging mistakes into the catalog instead of fixing them.
- PublicSignal 0360: source=TestingDoc; text=## What to Test Locally
- PublicSignal 0361: source=TestingDoc; text=Before you think about publishing, check:
- PublicSignal 0362: source=TestingDoc; text=does ADH detect the package
- PublicSignal 0363: source=TestingDoc; text=does the manifest parse correctly
- PublicSignal 0364: source=TestingDoc; text=does the mod appear with the expected name and version
- PublicSignal 0365: source=TestingDoc; text=does the DLL path resolve correctly
- PublicSignal 0366: source=TestingDoc; text=does the mod initialize without obvious failures
- PublicSignal 0367: source=TestingDoc; text=does the mod still behave correctly after a scene change
- PublicSignal 0368: source=TestingDoc; text=does the enable and disable state behave normally
- PublicSignal 0369: source=TestingDoc; text=does deleting and re-importing the package still work
- PublicSignal 0370: source=TestingDoc; text=## Zip Import Expectations
- PublicSignal 0371: source=TestingDoc; text=For a standard DLL mod, ADH expects a zip like this:
- PublicSignal 0372: source=TestingDoc; text=```text
- PublicSignal 0373: source=TestingDoc; text=MyCoolMod.zip
- PublicSignal 0374: source=TestingDoc; text=adh-manifest.json
- PublicSignal 0375: source=TestingDoc; text=MyCoolMod.dll
- PublicSignal 0376: source=TestingDoc; text=```
- PublicSignal 0377: source=TestingDoc; text=or this:
- PublicSignal 0378: source=TestingDoc; text=```text
- PublicSignal 0379: source=TestingDoc; text=MyCoolMod.zip
- PublicSignal 0380: source=TestingDoc; text=MyCoolMod/
- PublicSignal 0381: source=TestingDoc; text=adh-manifest.json
- PublicSignal 0382: source=TestingDoc; text=MyCoolMod.dll
- PublicSignal 0383: source=TestingDoc; text=```
- PublicSignal 0384: source=TestingDoc; text=That is enough for a normal package.
- PublicSignal 0385: source=TestingDoc; text=If your zip contains several possible manifests, ADH may ask which one should be used.
- PublicSignal 0386: source=TestingDoc; text=That can be useful in some deliberate bundle cases, but most of the time it means the archive was not kept clean enough.
- PublicSignal 0387: source=TestingDoc; text=## What Not to Upload
- PublicSignal 0388: source=TestingDoc; text=Do not upload:
- PublicSignal 0389: source=TestingDoc; text=placeholder packages
- PublicSignal 0390: source=TestingDoc; text=empty shells
- PublicSignal 0391: source=TestingDoc; text=notes-only zips
- PublicSignal 0392: source=TestingDoc; text=support files with no real payload
- PublicSignal 0393: source=TestingDoc; text=builds with version numbers that do not match the actual package
- PublicSignal 0394: source=TestingDoc; text=archives where the manifest points to files that are not present
- PublicSignal 0395: source=TestingDoc; text=## Uploading Through ADH
- PublicSignal 0396: source=TestingDoc; text=The normal developer flow is:
- PublicSignal 0397: source=TestingDoc; text=1. open ADH
- PublicSignal 0398: source=TestingDoc; text=2. go to the developer section
- PublicSignal 0399: source=TestingDoc; text=3. create or open a project
- PublicSignal 0400: source=TestingDoc; text=4. enter the project title
- PublicSignal 0401: source=TestingDoc; text=5. enter the description
- PublicSignal 0402: source=TestingDoc; text=6. add an optional website URL if needed
- PublicSignal 0403: source=TestingDoc; text=7. upload the package
- PublicSignal 0404: source=TestingDoc; text=The upload still needs to be a valid ADH package.
- PublicSignal 0405: source=TestingDoc; text=Uploading is not magic.
- PublicSignal 0406: source=TestingDoc; text=It does not fix:
- PublicSignal 0407: source=TestingDoc; text=bad pathing
- PublicSignal 0408: source=TestingDoc; text=bad DLL builds
- PublicSignal 0409: source=TestingDoc; text=invalid JSON
- PublicSignal 0410: source=TestingDoc; text=wrong package shape
- PublicSignal 0411: source=TestingDoc; text=missing payload files
- PublicSignal 0412: source=TestingDoc; text=If the package is bad locally, it is still bad after upload.
- PublicSignal 0413: source=TestingDoc; text=## Official and Unofficial Content
- PublicSignal 0414: source=TestingDoc; text=User projects should be treated as unofficial by default unless the ADH side explicitly marks them as official.
- PublicSignal 0415: source=TestingDoc; text=That matters for user trust.
- PublicSignal 0416: source=TestingDoc; text=If you are not publishing first-party ADH content, assume:
- PublicSignal 0417: source=TestingDoc; text=`trust_level = "Unofficial"`
- PublicSignal 0418: source=TestingDoc; text=Do not mark unofficial content as official because it â€œseems fine.â€
- PublicSignal 0419: source=TestingDoc; text=Trust labels should stay deliberate.
- PublicSignal 0420: source=TestingDoc; text=## Packs
- PublicSignal 0421: source=TestingDoc; text=Packs are grouped installs.
- PublicSignal 0422: source=TestingDoc; text=Use a pack when the package is meant to deliver multiple related pieces together, not when you are just shipping one normal DLL mod.
- PublicSignal 0423: source=TestingDoc; text=Example:
- PublicSignal 0424: source=TestingDoc; text=```json
- PublicSignal 0425: source=TestingDoc; text={
- PublicSignal 0426: source=TestingDoc; text="id": "my-pack",
- PublicSignal 0427: source=TestingDoc; text="name": "My Pack",
- PublicSignal 0428: source=TestingDoc; text="version": "1.0.0",
- PublicSignal 0429: source=TestingDoc; text="author": "Your Name",
- PublicSignal 0430: source=TestingDoc; text="description": "Bundle of mods.",
- PublicSignal 0431: source=TestingDoc; text="entry": "",
- PublicSignal 0432: source=TestingDoc; text="enabled": "true",
- PublicSignal 0433: source=TestingDoc; text="kind": "pack",
- PublicSignal 0434: source=TestingDoc; text="priority": "0",
- PublicSignal 0435: source=TestingDoc; text="loader_version": "2.0.0",
- PublicSignal 0436: source=TestingDoc; text="source_url": "",
- PublicSignal 0437: source=TestingDoc; text="download_url": "",
- PublicSignal 0438: source=TestingDoc; text="category": "Pack",
- PublicSignal 0439: source=TestingDoc; text="trust_level": "Unofficial",
- PublicSignal 0440: source=TestingDoc; text="testing_status": ""
- PublicSignal 0441: source=TestingDoc; text=}
- PublicSignal 0442: source=TestingDoc; text=```
- PublicSignal 0443: source=TestingDoc; text=If you are shipping one normal runtime mod, use `kind = "code"`.
- PublicSignal 0444: source=TestingDoc; text=## Common Packaging Mistakes Found Late
- PublicSignal 0445: source=TestingDoc; text=A lot of upload failures are not really â€œuploadâ€ failures.
- PublicSignal 0446: source=TestingDoc; text=They are packaging mistakes discovered late.
- PublicSignal 0447: source=TestingDoc; text=Typical examples:
- PublicSignal 0448: source=TestingDoc; text=manifest file name is wrong
- PublicSignal 0449: source=TestingDoc; text=DLL path does not match `entry`
- PublicSignal 0450: source=TestingDoc; text=the correct DLL was not included in the zip
- PublicSignal 0451: source=TestingDoc; text=the JSON is malformed
- PublicSignal 0452: source=TestingDoc; text=the mod `id` changed accidentally between releases
- PublicSignal 0453: source=TestingDoc; text=the version did not match the actual build
- PublicSignal 0454: source=TestingDoc; text=the archive contains random junk from the build tree
- PublicSignal 0455: source=FailureDoc; text=# ADH Aestik Modding Guide Part 5
- PublicSignal 0456: source=FailureDoc; text=## Common Failures, Checklists, and Best Practices
- PublicSignal 0457: source=FailureDoc; text=This section is the practical support layer.
- PublicSignal 0458: source=FailureDoc; text=It focuses on what usually goes wrong, what to check before release, and what habits make ADH mods more reliable over time.
- PublicSignal 0459: source=FailureDoc; text=## Common Failure Points
- PublicSignal 0460: source=FailureDoc; text=These are the most common reasons an ADH mod fails to work:
- PublicSignal 0461: source=FailureDoc; text=wrong manifest filename
- PublicSignal 0462: source=FailureDoc; text=wrong `entry` path
- PublicSignal 0463: source=FailureDoc; text=missing DLL
- PublicSignal 0464: source=FailureDoc; text=invalid JSON
- PublicSignal 0465: source=FailureDoc; text=reused or unstable `id`
- PublicSignal 0466: source=FailureDoc; text=wrong runtime references
- PublicSignal 0467: source=FailureDoc; text=heavy startup behavior that harms early game flow
- PublicSignal 0468: source=FailureDoc; text=scene-dependent logic that assumes too much too early
- PublicSignal 0469: source=FailureDoc; text=event listeners that are never cleaned up
- PublicSignal 0470: source=FailureDoc; text=UI elements that get created more than once
- PublicSignal 0471: source=FailureDoc; text=These failures often look different from each other on the surface, but most of them reduce to the same root problem.
- PublicSignal 0472: source=FailureDoc; text=Either the package is not describing the payload correctly, or the runtime code is making assumptions that do not survive real gameplay.
- PublicSignal 0473: source=FailureDoc; text=## What to Avoid
- PublicSignal 0474: source=FailureDoc; text=Do not depend on random manifest names.
- PublicSignal 0475: source=FailureDoc; text=Do not expect ADH to run shell commands from the manifest.
- PublicSignal 0476: source=FailureDoc; text=Do not hide the DLL under messy deep nesting unless the pathing is intentional and necessary.
- PublicSignal 0477: source=FailureDoc; text=Do not reuse someone elseâ€™s `id`.
- PublicSignal 0478: source=FailureDoc; text=Do not upload empty or placeholder packages.
- PublicSignal 0479: source=FailureDoc; text=Do not ship a version number that does not match the build users are actually getting.
- PublicSignal 0480: source=FailureDoc; text=Do not write startup logic that assumes every scene object already exists.
- PublicSignal 0481: source=FailureDoc; text=Do not treat a successful import as proof that the mod actually works.
- PublicSignal 0482: source=FailureDoc; text=## Good Habits
- PublicSignal 0483: source=FailureDoc; text=Keep the folder name close to the mod id.
- PublicSignal 0484: source=FailureDoc; text=Keep the DLL name obvious.
- PublicSignal 0485: source=FailureDoc; text=Keep the description short and honest.
- PublicSignal 0486: source=FailureDoc; text=Keep the manifest exact and valid JSON.
- PublicSignal 0487: source=FailureDoc; text=Test with a local zip before uploading online.
- PublicSignal 0488: source=FailureDoc; text=Keep version numbers updated.
- PublicSignal 0489: source=FailureDoc; text=Keep startup logic as light as practical.
- PublicSignal 0490: source=FailureDoc; text=Use an already working ADH mod as a structural reference when you are unsure how to shape your own.
- PublicSignal 0491: source=FailureDoc; text=## Fast Package Checklist
- PublicSignal 0492: source=FailureDoc; text=Before importing or uploading, check:
- PublicSignal 0493: source=FailureDoc; text=1. does the zip contain `adh-manifest.json` or `manifest.json`
- PublicSignal 0494: source=FailureDoc; text=2. does the manifest point to the correct DLL
- PublicSignal 0495: source=FailureDoc; text=3. does the DLL actually exist in the archive
- PublicSignal 0496: source=FailureDoc; text=4. is the JSON valid
- PublicSignal 0497: source=FailureDoc; text=5. is the `id` unique
- PublicSignal 0498: source=FailureDoc; text=6. does the version match the build
- PublicSignal 0499: source=FailureDoc; text=7. is the package actually a mod rather than just support files
- PublicSignal 0500: source=FailureDoc; text=## Fast Runtime Checklist
- PublicSignal 0501: source=FailureDoc; text=Before release, also check:
- PublicSignal 0502: source=FailureDoc; text=1. does the mod initialize safely at startup
- PublicSignal 0503: source=FailureDoc; text=2. does it still behave correctly after a scene change
- PublicSignal 0504: source=FailureDoc; text=3. does it assume objects exist too early
- PublicSignal 0505: source=FailureDoc; text=4. does it clean up event hooks or persistent objects properly
- PublicSignal 0506: source=FailureDoc; text=5. does it avoid unnecessary heavy startup work
- PublicSignal 0507: source=FailureDoc; text=## Example Release Layout
- PublicSignal 0508: source=FailureDoc; text=The most common expected release layout is:
- PublicSignal 0509: source=FailureDoc; text=```text
- PublicSignal 0510: source=FailureDoc; text=ExampleMod/
- PublicSignal 0511: source=FailureDoc; text=adh-manifest.json
- PublicSignal 0512: source=FailureDoc; text=ExampleMod.dll
- PublicSignal 0513: source=FailureDoc; text=```
- PublicSignal 0514: source=FailureDoc; text=That is the cleanest baseline for a standard DLL mod.
- PublicSignal 0515: source=FailureDoc; text=## Final Advice
- PublicSignal 0516: source=FailureDoc; text=If you want your mod to work well with both ADH and Aestik, think in this order:
- PublicSignal 0517: source=FailureDoc; text=1. is the package valid
- PublicSignal 0518: source=FailureDoc; text=2. is the manifest correct
- PublicSignal 0519: source=FailureDoc; text=3. is the DLL built properly
- PublicSignal 0520: source=FailureDoc; text=4. does the runtime behavior make sense during early startup
- PublicSignal 0521: source=FailureDoc; text=5. does the mod still behave correctly after the game enters normal play
- PublicSignal 0522: source=FailureDoc; text=That order matters because it mirrors the way real failures usually appear.
- PublicSignal 0523: source=FailureDoc; text=You cannot debug runtime behavior correctly if the package is fundamentally wrong.
- PublicSignal 0524: source=FailureDoc; text=You cannot judge package quality correctly if the runtime behavior is unstable after scene transitions.
- PublicSignal 0525: source=FailureDoc; text=Treat packaging and runtime behavior as one connected system.
- PublicSignal 0526: source=FailureDoc; text=That mindset will save you a lot of time.
- PublicSignal 0527: source=FailureDoc; text=## The Most Useful Standard
- PublicSignal 0528: source=FailureDoc; text=The best real standard is not whether the mod â€œworks once.â€
- PublicSignal 0529: source=FailureDoc; text=The best standard is whether the mod still behaves correctly in repeated real use.
- PublicSignal 0530: source=FailureDoc; text=That means:
- PublicSignal 0531: source=FailureDoc; text=it imports cleanly
- PublicSignal 0532: source=FailureDoc; text=it launches cleanly
- PublicSignal 0533: source=FailureDoc; text=it survives scene changes
- PublicSignal 0534: source=FailureDoc; text=it behaves predictably when enabled and disabled
- PublicSignal 0535: source=FailureDoc; text=it is packaged clearly enough that another person can inspect it and understand what it is
- PublicSignal 0536: source=FailureDoc; text=If you can meet that standard, your mod is in good shape.
- PublicSignal 0537: source=ApprovalDoc; text=# Contribution and Approval Process
- PublicSignal 0538: source=ApprovalDoc; text=Thank you for your interest in contributing to ADH.
- PublicSignal 0539: source=ApprovalDoc; text=## Contributing to ADH
- PublicSignal 0540: source=ApprovalDoc; text=Anyone may submit changes, improvements, bug fixes, or new features through a Pull Request.
- PublicSignal 0541: source=ApprovalDoc; text=Submitting a Pull Request does not guarantee it will be accepted. All contributions are reviewed before being merged into ADH.
- PublicSignal 0542: source=ApprovalDoc; text=Reasons a contribution may be rejected include:
- PublicSignal 0543: source=ApprovalDoc; text=* Malicious code or security concerns.
- PublicSignal 0544: source=ApprovalDoc; text=* Broken functionality.
- PublicSignal 0545: source=ApprovalDoc; text=* Poor code quality.
- PublicSignal 0546: source=ApprovalDoc; text=* Features that do not fit the project's goals.
- PublicSignal 0547: source=ApprovalDoc; text=* Incomplete or untested changes.
- PublicSignal 0548: source=ApprovalDoc; text=## Questions
- PublicSignal 0549: source=ApprovalDoc; text=If you have questions about contributing, a Pull Request, or a decision regarding your contribution, you can contact us on Discord.
- PublicSignal 0550: source=ApprovalDoc; text=Discord Username: tomcreations_owner
- PublicSignal 0551: source=ApprovalDoc; text=We are generally open to discussion and are willing to review additional information when needed.
- PublicSignal 0552: source=ApprovalDoc; text=## Community Mods
- PublicSignal 0553: source=ApprovalDoc; text=Community mod support is planned but is not currently available.
- PublicSignal 0554: source=ApprovalDoc; text=In the future, users will be able to upload mods through the ADH application.
- PublicSignal 0555: source=ApprovalDoc; text=When community mods become available:
- PublicSignal 0556: source=ApprovalDoc; text=* Uploaded mods may be removed at any time.
- PublicSignal 0557: source=ApprovalDoc; text=* All new mods will receive an Unofficial tag by default.
- PublicSignal 0558: source=ApprovalDoc; text=* Mods may later be reviewed and marked as Approved.
- PublicSignal 0559: source=ApprovalDoc; text=* Mods may also be removed if they violate guidelines, contain harmful content, or are otherwise unsuitable for the platform.
- PublicSignal 0560: source=ApprovalDoc; text=Approval does not guarantee that a mod is safe, bug-free, or compatible with every system.
- PublicSignal 0561: source=ApprovalDoc; text=Users install community mods at their own risk.
- PublicSignal 0562: source=ApprovalDoc; text=## Final Decisions
- PublicSignal 0563: source=ApprovalDoc; text=The ADH team reserves the right to accept, reject, remove, or modify contributions and community content when necessary for the health, security, and quality of the project.
- PublicSignal 0564: source=ManifestSpec; text=# ADH Manifest Specification
- PublicSignal 0565: source=ManifestSpec; text=Version: `1.0`
- PublicSignal 0566: source=ManifestSpec; text=This document defines the required JSON manifest format for ADH mods.
- PublicSignal 0567: source=ManifestSpec; text=## Goals
- PublicSignal 0568: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Make every mod declare what it is and what it installs.
- PublicSignal 0569: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Prevent arbitrary code execution during installation.
- PublicSignal 0570: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Keep dependency declarations visible to the user.
- PublicSignal 0571: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Allow the format to grow over time without breaking older mods.
- PublicSignal 0572: source=ManifestSpec; text=## File name
- PublicSignal 0573: source=ManifestSpec; text=Every mod package must contain a root manifest file named:
- PublicSignal 0574: source=ManifestSpec; text=`adh-manifest.json`
- PublicSignal 0575: source=ManifestSpec; text=If this file is missing, ADH must refuse installation.
- PublicSignal 0576: source=ManifestSpec; text=## Safety model
- PublicSignal 0577: source=ManifestSpec; text=ADH must treat the manifest as declarative data only.
- PublicSignal 0578: source=ManifestSpec; text=The manifest may describe:
- PublicSignal 0579: source=ManifestSpec; text=- metadata
- PublicSignal 0580: source=ManifestSpec; text=- compatibility
- PublicSignal 0581: source=ManifestSpec; text=- files to copy
- PublicSignal 0582: source=ManifestSpec; text=- folders to create
- PublicSignal 0583: source=ManifestSpec; text=- optional external dependencies that the user may install manually
- PublicSignal 0584: source=ManifestSpec; text=The manifest must not be able to:
- PublicSignal 0585: source=ManifestSpec; text=- run shell commands
- PublicSignal 0586: source=ManifestSpec; text=- run PowerShell, batch, Python, Node, or other scripts
- PublicSignal 0587: source=ManifestSpec; text=- modify the registry
- PublicSignal 0588: source=ManifestSpec; text=- download and execute binaries
- PublicSignal 0589: source=ManifestSpec; text=- patch arbitrary paths outside approved game and mod-loader roots
- PublicSignal 0590: source=ManifestSpec; text=## Top-level format
- PublicSignal 0591: source=ManifestSpec; text=```json
- PublicSignal 0592: source=ManifestSpec; text={
- PublicSignal 0593: source=ManifestSpec; text="manifestVersion": "1.0",
- PublicSignal 0594: source=ManifestSpec; text="id": "example.mod",
- PublicSignal 0595: source=ManifestSpec; text="name": "Example Mod",
- PublicSignal 0596: source=ManifestSpec; text="version": "1.0.0",
- PublicSignal 0597: source=ManifestSpec; text="author": "Example Author",
- PublicSignal 0598: source=ManifestSpec; text="description": "Short mod summary.",
- PublicSignal 0599: source=ManifestSpec; text="homepage": "https://example.com/mod",
- PublicSignal 0600: source=ManifestSpec; text="support": {
- PublicSignal 0601: source=ManifestSpec; text="game": "Aestik",
- PublicSignal 0602: source=ManifestSpec; text="gameVersions": ["1.0.0", "1.0.1"],
- PublicSignal 0603: source=ManifestSpec; text="loaderVersion": ">=1.0.0"
- PublicSignal 0604: source=ManifestSpec; text=},
- PublicSignal 0605: source=ManifestSpec; text="classification": {
- PublicSignal 0606: source=ManifestSpec; text="channel": "unofficial",
- PublicSignal 0607: source=ManifestSpec; text="reviewedByAdh": false
- PublicSignal 0608: source=ManifestSpec; text=},
- PublicSignal 0609: source=ManifestSpec; text="dependencies": [],
- PublicSignal 0610: source=ManifestSpec; text="install": {
- PublicSignal 0611: source=ManifestSpec; text="createFolders": [],
- PublicSignal 0612: source=ManifestSpec; text="copyFiles": []
- PublicSignal 0613: source=ManifestSpec; text=},
- PublicSignal 0614: source=ManifestSpec; text="entrypoints": [],
- PublicSignal 0615: source=ManifestSpec; text="capabilities": [],
- PublicSignal 0616: source=ManifestSpec; text="custom": {}
- PublicSignal 0617: source=ManifestSpec; text=}
- PublicSignal 0618: source=ManifestSpec; text=```
- PublicSignal 0619: source=ManifestSpec; text=## Required fields
- PublicSignal 0620: source=ManifestSpec; text=### `manifestVersion`
- PublicSignal 0621: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Type: string
- PublicSignal 0622: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Required: yes
- PublicSignal 0623: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Current value: `1.0`
- PublicSignal 0624: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Purpose: allows ADH to parse the document according to a specific contract.
- PublicSignal 0625: source=ManifestSpec; text=ADH rules:
- PublicSignal 0626: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Reject manifests with no `manifestVersion`.
- PublicSignal 0627: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Reject manifests with a newer major version that ADH does not understand.
- PublicSignal 0628: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Ignore unknown fields from the same major version.
- PublicSignal 0629: source=ManifestSpec; text=### `id`
- PublicSignal 0630: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Type: string
- PublicSignal 0631: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Required: yes
- PublicSignal 0632: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Pattern: reverse-domain-like or dotted identifier, such as `com.author.modname` or `author.modname`
- PublicSignal 0633: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Length: `3-128`
- PublicSignal 0634: source=ManifestSpec; text=ADH rules:
- PublicSignal 0635: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Must be unique per installed mod.
- PublicSignal 0636: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Must use only lowercase letters, digits, dots, dashes, and underscores.
- PublicSignal 0637: source=ManifestSpec; text=- If a new install conflicts with an existing `id`, ADH must ask whether to replace, cancel, or install as a separate disabled staging copy.
- PublicSignal 0638: source=ManifestSpec; text=### `name`
- PublicSignal 0639: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Type: string
- PublicSignal 0640: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Required: yes
- PublicSignal 0641: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Length: `1-120`
- PublicSignal 0642: source=ManifestSpec; text=### `version`
- PublicSignal 0643: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Type: string
- PublicSignal 0644: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Required: yes
- PublicSignal 0645: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Recommended: semantic versioning, for example `1.4.2`
- PublicSignal 0646: source=ManifestSpec; text=ADH rules:
- PublicSignal 0647: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Store as text.
- PublicSignal 0648: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Compare semver when possible.
- PublicSignal 0649: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Fall back to string comparison only for display, not upgrade logic.
- PublicSignal 0650: source=ManifestSpec; text=### `author`
- PublicSignal 0651: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Type: string
- PublicSignal 0652: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Required: yes
- PublicSignal 0653: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Length: `1-120`
- PublicSignal 0654: source=ManifestSpec; text=### `description`
- PublicSignal 0655: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Type: string
- PublicSignal 0656: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Required: yes
- PublicSignal 0657: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Length: `1-2000`
- PublicSignal 0658: source=ManifestSpec; text=## Optional metadata fields
- PublicSignal 0659: source=ManifestSpec; text=### `homepage`
- PublicSignal 0660: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Type: string
- PublicSignal 0661: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Optional
- PublicSignal 0662: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Must be `https://` if present
- PublicSignal 0663: source=ManifestSpec; text=### `support`
- PublicSignal 0664: source=ManifestSpec; text=Required object fields:
- PublicSignal 0665: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `game`
- PublicSignal 0666: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `gameVersions`
- PublicSignal 0667: source=ManifestSpec; text=Optional object fields:
- PublicSignal 0668: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `loaderVersion`
- PublicSignal 0669: source=ManifestSpec; text=Rules:
- PublicSignal 0670: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `game` must be `Aestik`
- PublicSignal 0671: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `gameVersions` is a non-empty array of supported versions or version ranges
- PublicSignal 0672: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `loaderVersion` is a version range string for ADH compatibility
- PublicSignal 0673: source=ManifestSpec; text=ADH processing:
- PublicSignal 0674: source=ManifestSpec; text=- If the current game version is unsupported, show a compatibility warning and require explicit confirmation before install.
- PublicSignal 0675: source=ManifestSpec; text=- If the loader version is unsupported, block install.
- PublicSignal 0676: source=ManifestSpec; text=### `classification`
- PublicSignal 0677: source=ManifestSpec; text=Optional object for trust presentation.
- PublicSignal 0678: source=ManifestSpec; text=Fields:
- PublicSignal 0679: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `channel`: `official` or `unofficial`
- PublicSignal 0680: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `reviewedByAdh`: boolean
- PublicSignal 0681: source=ManifestSpec; text=ADH processing:
- PublicSignal 0682: source=ManifestSpec; text=- Official status must not be trusted solely from the manifest.
- PublicSignal 0683: source=ManifestSpec; text=- ADH server-side catalog or local trusted metadata must be the source of truth.
- PublicSignal 0684: source=ManifestSpec; text=- If a manifest says `official` but ADH does not verify it, display it as unofficial.
- PublicSignal 0685: source=ManifestSpec; text=## Dependencies
- PublicSignal 0686: source=ManifestSpec; text=Dependencies are declarations only.
- PublicSignal 0687: source=ManifestSpec; text=ADH must never install them automatically.
- PublicSignal 0688: source=ManifestSpec; text=### Format
- PublicSignal 0689: source=ManifestSpec; text=```json
- PublicSignal 0690: source=ManifestSpec; text="dependencies": [
- PublicSignal 0691: source=ManifestSpec; text={
- PublicSignal 0692: source=ManifestSpec; text="id": "python.requests",
- PublicSignal 0693: source=ManifestSpec; text="name": "requests",
- PublicSignal 0694: source=ManifestSpec; text="source": "pip",
- PublicSignal 0695: source=ManifestSpec; text="version": ">=2.32.0",
- PublicSignal 0696: source=ManifestSpec; text="required": true,
- PublicSignal 0697: source=ManifestSpec; text="reviewedByAdh": false,
- PublicSignal 0698: source=ManifestSpec; text="notes": "Needed for optional web export features."
- PublicSignal 0699: source=ManifestSpec; text=}
- PublicSignal 0700: source=ManifestSpec; text=]
- PublicSignal 0701: source=ManifestSpec; text=```
- PublicSignal 0702: source=ManifestSpec; text=### Supported `source` values
- PublicSignal 0703: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `pip`
- PublicSignal 0704: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `npm`
- PublicSignal 0705: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `executable`
- PublicSignal 0706: source=ManifestSpec; text=- `runtime`
